import {recordDocument} from './referral-record-viewer.mjs?v=4960';
import {referralPageHtml} from './referral-form.mjs?v=4970';
const status=document.querySelector('[data-network-status]'),selector=document.querySelector('[data-network-peer]'),search=document.querySelector('[data-network-search]'),compose=document.querySelector('[data-network-compose]');let peers=[],doctor=false,queryGeneration=0,results=[],resultPeer='',pendingMessage=null,templatePromise;
const query=()=>new URLSearchParams({peerId:selector.value});
async function messages(){if(!doctor)return;try{const data=await api('/api/emr-network/messages');document.querySelector('[data-network-messages]').innerHTML=data.messages.map(m=>'<article class="network-private-message"><strong>'+esc(m.facility)+' · '+esc(m.sender_name)+' → '+esc(m.recipient_name)+'</strong><p>'+esc(m.subject)+' · '+esc(new Date(m.created_at).toLocaleString('km-KH'))+'</p><div class="network-message">'+esc(m.body)+'</div><p>'+(m.state==='DELIVERED'?'បានបញ្ជូន':'កំពុងរង់ចាំបញ្ជូន')+'</p></article>').join('')}catch(error){status.textContent=safeError(error)}}
async function choose(){const ticket=++queryGeneration;results=[];document.querySelector('[data-network-results]').replaceChildren();compose.elements.recipient.replaceChildren();const peer=peers.find(p=>p.id===selector.value);search.querySelector('button').disabled=!peer?.search;compose.querySelector('button').disabled=!peer?.messages;
 if(doctor&&peer?.messages)try{const data=await api('/api/emr-network/doctors?'+query());if(ticket!==queryGeneration)return;compose.elements.recipient.innerHTML=data.doctors.map(d=>'<option value="'+esc(d.id)+'">'+esc(d.display_name)+'</option>').join('')}catch(error){status.textContent=safeError(error)}
}
selector.onchange=choose;
search.onsubmit=async event=>{event.preventDefault();const ticket=++queryGeneration,peerId=selector.value;status.textContent='កំពុងផ្ទុក';try{const params=new URLSearchParams({peerId,q:search.elements.q.value});const data=await api('/api/emr-network/search?'+params);if(ticket!==queryGeneration)return;results=data.patients;resultPeer=peerId;document.querySelector('[data-network-results]').innerHTML='<table><thead><tr><th>ឈ្មោះអ្នកជំងឺ</th><th>លេខបណ្ណសុខភាព</th><th>កាលបរិច្ឆេទចូល</th><th>សកម្មភាព</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+results.map((p,i)=>'<tr><td>'+esc(p.fullName)+'</td><td>'+esc(p.healthId)+'</td><td>'+esc(p.entryDate)+'</td><td><button type="button" data-network-view="'+i+'">មើល</button></td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table>';status.textContent='មើលបានតែប៉ុណ្ណោះ'}catch(error){status.textContent=safeError(error)}};
async function review(patient,peerId,trigger){
 const overlay=document.createElement('section');overlay.className='referral-modal';overlay.setAttribute('role','dialog');overlay.setAttribute('aria-modal','true');overlay.innerHTML='<div class="ref-toolbar"><strong>'+esc(patient.fullName)+'</strong><span>មើលបានតែប៉ុណ្ណោះ</span><select data-visit></select><select data-document><option value="medical">កំណត់ត្រាវេជ្ជសាស្ត្រ</option><option value="opd">ទម្រង់ពិគ្រោះក្រៅ</option><option value="nursing">តារាងថែទាំ</option><option value="referral">លិខិតបញ្ជូន</option></select><button type="button" data-close>បិទ</button><p role="status"></p></div><div class="ref-workspace" data-document-content></div>';
 const close=()=>{overlay.remove();trigger.focus()};overlay.querySelector('[data-close]').onclick=close;overlay.onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Escape')close();if(e.key==='Tab'){const nodes=[...overlay.querySelectorAll('button:not(:disabled),select:not(:disabled),iframe')].filter(n=>n.checkVisibility()),first=nodes[0],last=nodes.at(-1);if(e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===first){e.preventDefault();last.focus()}else if(!e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===last){e.preventDefault();first.focus()}}};document.body.append(overlay);overlay.querySelector('[data-close]').focus();const notice=overlay.querySelector('[role=status]'),visitSelect=overlay.querySelector('[data-visit]'),kind=overlay.querySelector('[data-document]'),content=overlay.querySelector('[data-document-content]');let visits=[],generation=0;
 const show=async()=>{const ticket=++generation,visit=visits[Number(visitSelect.value)];content.replaceChildren();if(!visit)return;if(kind.value==='referral'){if(!visit.form){content.textContent='មិនមានលិខិតបញ្ជូនសម្រាប់ការមកពិនិត្យនេះ';return}content.innerHTML=referralPageHtml(visit.form.data,visit.form.signature);content.querySelectorAll('input,textarea').forEach(n=>n.disabled=true);return}if((kind.value==='opd'&&visit.patient.serviceMode!=='opd')||(kind.value==='medical'&&visit.patient.serviceMode==='opd')){content.textContent='មិនមានទម្រង់នេះសម្រាប់ការមកពិនិត្យដែលបានជ្រើសរើស';return}try{templatePromise||=api('/api/referral-record-template').then(r=>r.html).catch(e=>{templatePromise=null;throw e});const template=await templatePromise;if(!overlay.isConnected||ticket!==generation)return;const frame=document.createElement('iframe');frame.className='network-record-frame';frame.setAttribute('sandbox','allow-same-origin');frame.title=kind.selectedOptions[0].textContent;frame.srcdoc=recordDocument(template,visit,kind.value);content.append(frame)}catch(error){notice.textContent=safeError(error)}};
 visitSelect.onchange=show;kind.onchange=show;
 try{notice.textContent='កំពុងផ្ទុក';const data=await api('/api/emr-network/records?'+new URLSearchParams({peerId,patientKey:patient.patientKey}));if(!overlay.isConnected)return;visits=data.visits;visitSelect.innerHTML=visits.map((v,i)=>'<option value="'+i+'">'+esc(v.patient.entryDate||'—')+' → '+esc(v.patient.dischargeDate||'—')+' · '+esc(v.patient.caseNumber)+'</option>').join('');const selected=visits.findIndex(v=>v.patient.patientKey===patient.patientKey);if(selected>=0)visitSelect.value=String(selected);kind.value=patient.serviceMode==='opd'?'opd':'medical';notice.textContent='មើលបានតែប៉ុណ្ណោះ';await show()}catch(error){notice.textContent=safeError(error)}
}
document.querySelector('[data-network-results]').onclick=event=>{const button=event.target.closest('[data-network-view]');if(button&&results[Number(button.dataset.networkView)])void review(results[Number(button.dataset.networkView)],resultPeer,button)};
compose.onsubmit=async event=>{event.preventDefault();const body={peerId:selector.value,recipientId:compose.elements.recipient.value,subject:compose.elements.subject.value,body:compose.elements.body.value},fingerprint=JSON.stringify(body);if(!pendingMessage||pendingMessage.fingerprint!==fingerprint)pendingMessage={fingerprint,id:crypto.randomUUID()};compose.querySelector('button').disabled=true;try{await api('/api/emr-network/messages',{...body,id:pendingMessage.id});pendingMessage=null;compose.elements.subject.value='';compose.elements.body.value='';status.textContent='បានបញ្ជូន';await messages()}catch(error){status.textContent=safeError(error)}finally{compose.querySelector('button').disabled=false}};
async function load(){try{const data=await api('/api/emr-network/connections');peers=data.peers;doctor=data.doctor;document.querySelector('[data-doctor-section]').hidden=!doctor;selector.innerHTML=peers.map(p=>'<option value="'+esc(p.id)+'">'+esc(p.name)+'</option>').join('');await choose();await messages()}catch(error){status.textContent=safeError(error)}}
void load();setInterval(messages,10000);

===== BEGIN FILE: public/emr-connections.mjs =====
import {api,esc,safeError} from './network-ui.mjs';
const status=document.querySelector('[data-network-status]'),localForm=document.querySelector('[data-local-form]'),peerForm=document.querySelector('[data-peer-form]');let settings,editing=null,busy=false;
async function load(){settings=await api('/api/emr-network/settings');localForm.elements.name.value=settings.local.name;localForm.elements.enabled.checked=settings.local.enabled;document.querySelector('[data-local-key-hint]').textContent=settings.local.keyHint;
 document.querySelector('[data-peer-list]').innerHTML=settings.peers.map(p=>'<article class="network-peer"><strong>'+esc(p.name)+'</strong><p>'+esc(p.origin)+' · '+esc(p.keyHint)+'</p><p>'+(p.enabled?'បានបើក':'បានបិទ')+'</p><button type="button" data-edit-peer="'+esc(p.id)+'">កែប្រែ</button> <button type="button" data-test-peer="'+esc(p.id)+'">សាកល្បងការតភ្ជាប់</button></article>').join('');
}
async function run(fn){if(busy)return;busy=true;try{await fn()}catch(e){status.textContent=safeError(e)}finally{busy=false}}
localForm.onsubmit=e=>{e.preventDefault();void run(async()=>{await api('/api/emr-network/local',{name:localForm.elements.name.value,enabled:localForm.elements.enabled.checked,version:settings.local.version});await load();status.textContent='បានរក្សាទុក'})};
document.querySelector('[data-copy-key]').onclick=()=>run(async()=>{const {apiKey}=await api('/api/emr-network/key',{});await navigator.clipboard.writeText(apiKey);status.textContent='បានចម្លងសោ'});
document.querySelector('[data-rotate-key]').onclick=()=>run(async()=>{if(!confirm('ការបង្កើតសោថ្មីនឹងផ្តាច់ការតភ្ជាប់ រហូតដល់មូលដ្ឋានផ្សេងធ្វើបច្ចុប្បន្នភាពសោ។'))return;await api('/api/emr-network/key',{rotate:true,version:settings.local.version});await load();status.textContent='បានបង្កើតសោថ្មី'})
document.querySelector('[data-peer-list]').onclick=e=>{const edit=e.target.closest('[data-edit-peer]'),test=e.target.closest('[data-test-peer]');if(edit){editing=settings.peers.find(p=>p.id===edit.dataset.editPeer);for(const k of ['name','origin'])peerForm.elements[k].value=editing[k];peerForm.elements.apiKey.value='';for(const k of ['enabled','incoming','outgoing','search','messages'])peerForm.elements[k].checked=editing[k];peerForm.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'})}if(test)void run(async()=>{status.textContent='កំពុងផ្ទុក';const {result}=await api('/api/emr-network/test',{peerId:test.dataset.testPeer});status.textContent=result.incoming?'ការតភ្ជាប់បានផ្ទៀងផ្ទាត់':'សោត្រឹមត្រូវ ប៉ុន្តែមូលដ្ឋានផ្សេងមិនអនុញ្ញាតសំណើចូល'})};
document.querySelector('[data-new-peer]').onclick=()=>{editing=null;peerForm.reset();peerForm.elements.name.focus()};
peerForm.onsubmit=e=>{e.preventDefault();void run(async()=>{const body={name:peerForm.elements.name.value,origin:peerForm.elements.origin.value,apiKey:peerForm.elements.apiKey.value};if(editing){body.id=editing.id;body.version=editing.version}for(const k of ['enabled','incoming','outgoing','search','messages'])body[k]=peerForm.elements[k].checked;await api('/api/emr-network/peers',body);peerForm.reset();editing=null;await load();status.textContent='បានរក្សាទុក'})};
void run(load);

===== BEGIN FILE: public/nav-config.js =====
/**
 * Borkeo EMR - central side navigation map.
 *
 * The ONE place that decides which sidebar buttons a user sees. Every rule
 * mirrors a page the server already allows (server/access.mjs roleCanPage and
 * the ward checks in server/server.mjs). Hiding a button is not security: the
 * server still rejects a direct URL for a role that cannot open the page.
 *
 * Access rules: 'ROLE' (any ward), 'ROLE@ward' (only that ward) or
 * 'ROLE@!ward' (any ward except that one).
 */
'use strict';

(function (root) {
  const MGMT = ['ADMIN', 'VICE_DIRECTOR', 'DIRECTOR'];
  const ALL_ROLES = ['ADMIN', 'VICE_DIRECTOR', 'DIRECTOR', 'REGISTRATION', 'TRIAGE', 'DOCTOR', 'NURSE', 'MIDWIFE',

    { id: 'billing', group: 'finance', href: '/billing', icon: 'icon-receipt', label: 'បេឡា និងវិក្កយបត្រ', labelEn: 'Billing', access: ['CLERK', 'ACCOUNTANT', ...MGMT] },
    { id: 'expenses', group: 'finance', href: '/expenses', icon: 'icon-wallet', label: 'កត់ត្រាចំណាយ', labelEn: 'Expenses', access: ['ACCOUNTANT', ...MGMT] },

    // Administration (requests stay management-only, v23.9 / v24.7)

    { id: 'admin-organization', group: 'admin', href: '/admin/organization', icon: 'icon-building', label: 'ឈ្មោះ និងស្លាកសញ្ញា', labelEn: 'Name and logo', access: MGMT },
    { id: 'admin-health-facility', group: 'admin', href: '/admin/health-facility', icon: 'icon-hospital', label: 'ព័ត៌មានមូលដ្ឋានសុខាភិបាល', labelEn: 'Health facility', access: MGMT },
    { id: 'admin-record-log', group: 'admin', href: '/admin/record-log', icon: 'icon-eye', label: 'កំណត់ត្រាអ្នកបើកមើលឯកសារ', labelEn: 'Record view log', access: MGMT },
    { id: 'admin-drug-safety', group: 'admin', href: '/admin/drug-safety', icon: 'icon-pill', label: '', newTextKey: 'nav.drugSafety', labelEn: 'Drug safety tables', access: MGMT },
    { id: 'admin-interface', group: 'admin', href: '/admin/interface', icon: 'icon-palette', label: 'រចនាគេហទំព័រ', labelEn: 'Interface design', access: MGMT },
    { id: 'styleguide', group: 'admin', href: '/styleguide', icon: 'icon-sliders', label: 'មគ្គុទ្ទេសក៍រចនាប័ទ្ម', labelEn: 'Styleguide', access: MGMT },
  ];

  ITEMS.push({ id:'admin-emr-connections',group:'admin',href:'/admin/emr-connections',icon:'icon-lock',label:'ការតភ្ជាប់ API រវាងមូលដ្ឋានសុខាភិបាល',labelEn:'EMR API connections',access:['ADMIN'] });

  function ruleMatches(rule, role, ward) {
    const [ruleRole, wardRule] = String(rule).split('@');
    if (ruleRole !== role) return false;
    if (!wardRule) return true;
    return wardRule.startsWith('!') ? ward !== wardRule.slice(1) : ward === wardRule;
  }

  // v42.1: the Superuser account gets exactly the menu of its active user
  // type, like a real account of that type.
  function itemVisible(item, user) {
    if(item.networkOnly && user?.referralNetwork!==true)return false;
    if (item.receiverOnly && user?.referralReceiver !== true) return false;
    const role = String(user?.activeRole || '').toUpperCase();
    const ward = String(user?.activeWard?.code || '').toLowerCase();
    return Boolean(role) && item.access.some(rule => ruleMatches(rule, role, ward));
  }

  // prefs: the admin "Interface -> navigation" settings for this role
  // ({ hidden: [href], order: [href] }). They can hide or reorder, never add.
  function visibleGroups(user, prefs) {
    const hidden = new Set(prefs?.hidden || []);
    const order = prefs?.order || [];
    const rank = href => { const i = order.indexOf(href); return i < 0 ? order.length : i; };
    return GROUPS.map(group => {
      const items = ITEMS
        .filter(item => item.group === group.id && itemVisible(item, user) && !hidden.has(item.href))
        .map((item, index) => ({ item, index }))
        .sort((a, b) => (rank(a.item.href) - rank(b.item.href)) || (a.index - b.index))
        .map(entry => entry.item);
      return { ...group, items };
    }).filter(group => group.items.length > 0);
  }

  function visibleItemIds(user, prefs) {
    return visibleGroups(user, prefs).flatMap(group => group.items.map(item => item.id));
  }

  // The best matching item for a URL: same path and every query parameter the
  // item declares; the most specific match wins.
  function activeItemId(items, pathname, search) {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(search || '');
    let best = null, bestScore = -1;
    for (const item of items) {
      const url = new URL(item.href, 'http://emr.local');
      if (url.pathname !== pathname) continue;
      const wanted = [...url.searchParams.entries()];
      if (!wanted.every(([key, value]) => params.get(key) === value)) continue;
      if (wanted.length > bestScore) { best = item.id; bestScore = wanted.length; }
    }
    return best;
  }

  root.EmrNavConfig = Object.freeze({ GROUPS, ITEMS, ALL_ROLES, itemVisible, visibleGroups, visibleItemIds, activeItemId });
})(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : globalThis);

===== END FILE: public/nav-config.js =====

export const esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
export const safeError=e=>/[\u1780-\u17ff]/.test(e?.message||'')?e.message:'មិនអាចផ្ទុកទិន្នន័យបាន';
export async function api(url,body){
 let csrf=window.emrSession?.csrfToken;if(body&&!csrf){const response=await fetch('/api/auth/me',{credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store'});if(response.status===401){location.assign('/login');throw Error('មិនមានសិទ្ធិ')}csrf=(await response.json()).csrfToken}
 const response=await fetch(url,{method:body?'POST':'GET',credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store',headers:body?{'Content-Type':'application/json','X-CSRF-Token':csrf}:{},body:body?JSON.stringify(body):undefined});
 const result=await response.json();if(!response.ok)throw Error(result.error||'មិនអាចផ្ទុកទិន្នន័យបាន');return result;
}

 <section class="ref-identity"><div class="ref-grid">${f('name')}${pair('sex','birth')}</div><div class="ref-grid">${pair('companion','phone')}${f('equity')}</div><div class="ref-grid ref-four">${['village','commune','districtAddress','provinceAddress'].map(k=>f(k)).join('')}</div></section>
 ${f('admission')}${f('complaint',true)}${f('history',true)}${f('exam',true)}
 <div class="ref-line">${f('results',true)}${c('noTests')}</div>${f('diagnosis',true)}

 <div class="ref-children"><div class="ref-box"><h2>កុមារអាយុក្រោម ៥ ឆ្នាំ</h2>${pair('childWeight','childHeight')}${f('childNutrition')}${f('childArm')}${f('childSd')}${f('childEdema')}${f('childBreast')}${f('childFood')}</div><div class="ref-box"><h2>ទារកអាយុក្រោម ៦ សប្តាហ៍</h2>${f('newbornBirth')}${f('newbornGestation')}${f('newbornDelivery')}${f('newbornWeight')}</div></div></section>

 }overlay.querySelector('[data-ref-print]').disabled=busy||!good||(!loaded.canEdit&&!stored?.signature);sendButton.disabled=busy||!loaded.canEdit||(peerPicker&&!peerPicker.value)||['PENDING','ACCEPTED','REJECTED'].includes(transfer?.status);return good}
 overlay.addEventListener('input',()=>{if(signature&&mode==='NONE'){signature=null;renderSignature('')}dirty=true;status(fits()?'':'អត្ថបទលើសទំហំសន្លឹក សូមសង្ខេបមុនបោះពុម្ព')});
 const save=async requireSignature=>{
  if(!loaded.canEdit)throw Error('មិនអាចកែប្រែទម្រង់នេះបានទេ');

 };
 const run=async fn=>{if(busy)return;busy=true;const controls=[...overlay.querySelectorAll('input,textarea,select,button')].map(n=>[n,n.disabled]);controls.forEach(([n])=>n.disabled=true);try{await fn()}catch(error){status(error.message)}finally{busy=false;controls.forEach(([n,disabled])=>n.disabled=disabled);if(transfer)showTransfer();else fits()}};

// Explicit bootstrap for the two owner-approved installations on this computer.
// Never prints keys or patient content. Does not create users or replace records.
import fs from 'node:fs';import path from 'node:path';import {pathToFileURL} from 'node:url';import {execFileSync} from 'node:child_process';import {DatabaseSync} from 'node:sqlite';
import {networkLocal,networkPeers,savePeer,apiToken,ensureNetworkSchema,networkCall} from '../server/emr-network.mjs';
import {ensureReferralLinkSchema,linkSettings} from '../server/referral-link.mjs';
const roots=process.argv.slice(2).map(r=>path.resolve(r));
if(roots.length!==2||roots[0]===roots[1])throw Error('Specify the two distinct approved installation roots.');
const configs=roots.map(root=>{
 const configUrl=pathToFileURL(path.join(root,'server/config.mjs')).href,wifiUrl=pathToFileURL(path.join(root,'scripts/wifi-instance.mjs')).href;
 const code=`const {loadConfig}=await import(${JSON.stringify(configUrl)});loadConfig();const {configureWifiInstance}=await import(${JSON.stringify(wifiUrl)});configureWifiInstance(${JSON.stringify(root)});const c=loadConfig();console.log(JSON.stringify({port:c.port,appOrigin:c.appOrigin,databasePath:c.databasePath}));`;
 return JSON.parse(execFileSync(process.execPath,['--input-type=module','-e',code],{cwd:root,encoding:'utf8',windowsHide:true}));
});
if(new Set(configs.map(c=>path.resolve(c.databasePath))).size!==2||!configs.every(c=>fs.existsSync(c.databasePath)))throw Error('Distinct existing databases are required.');
const legacy=configs.map(c=>linkSettings(c));
const migrateLegacy=legacy.every((l,i)=>l?.peer===configs[1-i].appOrigin)&&legacy[0].secret===legacy[1].secret;
const dbs=configs.map(c=>({sqlite:new DatabaseSync(c.databasePath)}));
try{
 dbs.forEach((db,i)=>{ensureReferralLinkSchema(db);ensureNetworkSchema(db,configs[i])});
 const prior=dbs.map(networkLocal);for(let i=0;i<2;i++)if(networkPeers(dbs[i]).some(p=>p.instance_id!==prior[1-i].id))throw Error('Other connections already exist; configure these installations through the admin page.');
 dbs.forEach(db=>db.sqlite.exec('BEGIN IMMEDIATE'));
 try{
  for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const name=process.env[i===0?'EMR_NETWORK_NAME_A':'EMR_NETWORK_NAME_B'];if(name&&!prior[i].managed){if(name.length>160)throw Error('Facility name too long.');dbs[i].sqlite.prepare('UPDATE emr_network_local SET name=?').run(name.trim())}}
  for(let i=0;i<2;i++){
   const db=dbs[i],other=networkLocal(dbs[1-i]),existing=networkPeers(db).find(p=>p.instance_id===other.id);
   const peer=savePeer(db,configs[i],{id:existing?.id,version:existing?.version,name:other.name,origin:configs[1-i].appOrigin,apiKey:apiToken(other),enabled:true,incoming:true,outgoing:true,search:true,messages:true});
   if(migrateLegacy)for(const table of ['referral_inbox','referral_outbox'])db.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE '+table+" SET peer_id=? WHERE peer_id=''").run(peer.id);
   db.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE emr_network_local SET enabled=1,managed=1,version=version+1').run();
  }
  dbs.forEach(db=>db.sqlite.exec('COMMIT'));
 }catch(error){for(const db of dbs)try{db.sqlite.exec('ROLLBACK')}catch{}throw error}
 for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const peer=networkPeers(dbs[i])[0],result=await networkCall({database:dbs[i],config:configs[i]},peer.id,{operation:'ping'},{probe:true});if(!result.incoming)throw Error('Remote approval not enabled.')}
 console.log('Two independent API keys and reciprocal permissions configured; signed identity checks passed. Legacy referral bindings preserved: '+migrateLegacy);
}finally{dbs.forEach(db=>db.sqlite.close())}

===== END FILE: scripts/pair-local-emrs.mjs =====

===== BEGIN FILE: server/emr-network.mjs =====
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import {linkSettings,peerCall} from './referral-link.mjs';
import {networkPublicKey} from './network-signatures.mjs';
const fail=(status,message)=>{throw Object.assign(new Error(message),{status})};
export const DOCTORS=['DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD'];
export const REVIEW_ROLES=['ADMIN','DIRECTOR','VICE_DIRECTOR','DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD','NURSE','MIDWIFE','REGISTRATION','TRIAGE'];
export function ensureNetworkSchema(db,config){
 db.sqlite.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS emr_network_local(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,secret TEXT NOT NULL,name TEXT NOT NULL,enabled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,managed INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1) STRICT;
 CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS emr_network_peers(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,instance_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,name TEXT NOT NULL,origin TEXT NOT NULL,secret TEXT NOT NULL,enabled INTEGER NOT NULL,incoming INTEGER NOT NULL,outgoing INTEGER NOT NULL,search INTEGER NOT NULL,messages INTEGER NOT NULL,version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1) STRICT;
 CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS emr_network_messages(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,peer_id TEXT NOT NULL,direction TEXT NOT NULL,sender_id TEXT NOT NULL,sender_name TEXT NOT NULL,recipient_id TEXT NOT NULL,recipient_name TEXT NOT NULL,subject TEXT NOT NULL,body TEXT NOT NULL,state TEXT NOT NULL,created_at INTEGER NOT NULL) STRICT;`);
 if(!db.sqlite.prepare('SELECT 1 FROM emr_network_local').get())db.sqlite.prepare('INSERT INTO emr_network_local(id,secret,name) VALUES(?,?,?)').run(crypto.randomUUID(),crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex'),'EMR '+config.port);
 if(!db.sqlite.prepare('PRAGMA table_info(emr_network_local)').all().some(c=>c.name==='signing_seed'))db.sqlite.exec("ALTER TABLE emr_network_local ADD COLUMN signing_seed TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''");
 db.sqlite.prepare("UPDATE emr_network_local SET signing_seed=? WHERE signing_seed=''").run(crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex'));
 for(const table of ['referral_inbox','referral_outbox'])if(!db.sqlite.prepare('PRAGMA table_info('+table+')').all().some(c=>c.name==='peer_id'))db.sqlite.exec("ALTER TABLE "+table+" ADD COLUMN peer_id TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''");
}
export const networkLocal=db=>db.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM emr_network_local').get();
export const networkPeers=db=>db.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM emr_network_peers ORDER BY name,id').all();
export const apiToken=local=>'EMR1:'+local.id+':'+networkPublicKey(local.signing_seed);
export function parseApiToken(value){const m=/^EMR1:([a-f0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{12}):([a-f0-9]{64})$/.exec(String(value||'').trim());if(!m)fail(400,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');return {id:m[1],secret:m[2]}}
export function publicPeer(p){return {id:p.id,instanceId:p.instance_id,name:p.name,origin:p.origin,enabled:!!p.enabled,incoming:!!p.incoming,outgoing:!!p.outgoing,search:!!p.search,messages:!!p.messages,version:p.version,keyHint:'••••'+p.secret.slice(-4)}}
export function savePeer(db,config,body){
 const local=networkLocal(db),prior=body.id?db.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM emr_network_peers WHERE id=?').get(String(body.id)):null;
 if(body.id&&!prior)fail(404,'រកមិនឃើញ');if(prior&&body.version!==prior.version)fail(409,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');
 const name=String(body.name||'').trim();if(!name||name.length>160)fail(400,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');
 let url;try{url=new URL(body.origin)}catch{fail(400,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ')}
 if(url.protocol!=='http:'||!['127.0.0.1','localhost'].includes(url.hostname)||!url.port||url.username||url.password||url.search||url.hash||url.pathname!=='/'||url.port===new URL(config.appOrigin).port)fail(400,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');
 const token=body.apiKey?parseApiToken(body.apiKey):prior?{id:prior.instance_id,secret:prior.secret}:parseApiToken('');
 if(token.id===local.id||(prior&&token.id!==prior.instance_id))fail(400,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');
 const flags={};for(const key of ['enabled','incoming','outgoing','search','messages']){if(body[key]!==undefined&&typeof body[key]!=='boolean')fail(400,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');flags[key]=body[key]===undefined?(prior?.[key]||0):Number(body[key])}
 const id=prior?.id||crypto.randomUUID();
 try{if(prior)db.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE emr_network_peers SET name=?,origin=?,secret=?,enabled=?,incoming=?,outgoing=?,search=?,messages=?,version=version+1 WHERE id=? AND version=?').run(name,url.origin,token.secret,flags.enabled,flags.incoming,flags.outgoing,flags.search,flags.messages,id,prior.version);
 else db.sqlite.prepare('INSERT INTO emr_network_peers(id,instance_id,name,origin,secret,enabled,incoming,outgoing,search,messages) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)').run(id,token.id,name,url.origin,token.secret,flags.enabled,flags.incoming,flags.outgoing,flags.search,flags.messages)}catch{fail(409,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ')}
 return db.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM emr_network_peers WHERE id=?').get(id);
}
export function pairLink(local,peer){
 const secret=crypto.createHash('sha256').update('emr-network:'+local.id+':'+peer.instance_id+':'+networkPublicKey(local.signing_seed)+':'+peer.secret).digest('hex');
 return {role:'sender',peer:peer.origin,secret,networkFrom:local.id,networkTo:peer.instance_id,signingSeed:local.signing_seed,peerVerifyKey:peer.secret};
}
export function incomingPairKey(local,peer){return crypto.createHash('sha256').update('emr-network:'+peer.instance_id+':'+local.id+':'+peer.secret+':'+networkPublicKey(local.signing_seed)).digest('hex')}
export async function networkCall(ctx,peerId,body,{probe=false}={}){
 const local=networkLocal(ctx.database),peer=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM emr_network_peers WHERE id=?').get(String(peerId||''));
 if(!local.enabled||!peer?.enabled||(!probe&&!peer.outgoing))fail(403,'មិនមានសិទ្ធិ');
 if(!probe&&['search','records'].includes(body.operation)&&!peer.search)fail(403,'មិនមានសិទ្ធិ');
 if(!probe&&['doctors','message'].includes(body.operation)&&!peer.messages)fail(403,'មិនមានសិទ្ធិ');
 const link=pairLink(local,peer);
 // Verify the actual remote EMR before sending any query, clinical data or text.
 const ping=await peerCall(link,{operation:'ping'});
 if(ping.instanceId!==peer.instance_id||ping.protocol!==1)fail(403,'មិនមានសិទ្ធិ');
 if(probe||body.operation==='ping')return ping;
 if(!ping.incoming||(['search','records'].includes(body.operation)&&!ping.search)||(['doctors','message'].includes(body.operation)&&!ping.messages))fail(403,'មិនមានសិទ្ធិ');
 return peerCall(link,body);
}
export function referralNetworkLink(ctx,peerId){
 const local=networkLocal(ctx.database);if(!local.managed)return linkSettings(ctx.config);
 const peers=networkPeers(ctx.database).filter(p=>p.enabled&&p.outgoing);const peer=peerId?peers.find(p=>p.id===peerId):peers.length===1?peers[0]:null;
 if(!local.enabled||!peer)fail(403,'មិនមានសិទ្ធិ');
 return {role:'sender',peerId:peer.id,invoke:body=>networkCall(ctx,peer.id,body)};
}
export function networkCapabilities(ctx){
 const local=networkLocal(ctx.database),peers=networkPeers(ctx.database);return {managed:!!local.managed,receiver:!!local.managed,sender:!!(local.enabled&&peers.some(p=>p.enabled&&p.outgoing)),outgoingPeers:local.enabled?peers.filter(p=>p.enabled&&p.outgoing).map(p=>({id:p.id,name:p.name})):[],network:!!local.managed};
}

===== END FILE: server/emr-network.mjs =====

===== BEGIN FILE: server/network-signatures.mjs =====
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
const privateKey=seed=>crypto.createPrivateKey({key:Buffer.from('302e020100300506032b657004220420'+seed,'hex'),format:'der',type:'pkcs8'});
export const networkPublicKey=seed=>crypto.createPublicKey(privateKey(seed)).export({format:'der',type:'spki'}).subarray(-32).toString('hex');
export const networkSign=(seed,value)=>crypto.sign(null,Buffer.from(value),privateKey(seed)).toString('hex');
export function networkVerify(publicHex,value,signature){
 if(!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(publicHex||'')||!/^[a-f0-9]{128}$/.test(signature||''))return false;
 try{return crypto.verify(null,Buffer.from(value),crypto.createPublicKey({key:Buffer.from('302a300506032b6570032100'+publicHex,'hex'),format:'der',type:'spki'}),Buffer.from(signature,'hex'))}catch{return false}
}
export const networkRequestText=e=>'emr-network-request:'+JSON.stringify({timestamp:e.timestamp,nonce:e.nonce,body:e.body,from:e.from,to:e.to});
export const networkResponseText=(nonce,body)=>'emr-network-response:'+nonce+JSON.stringify(body);

===== END FILE: server/network-signatures.mjs =====

===== BEGIN FILE: server/referral-link.mjs =====
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import {latestReferral} from './referral-forms.mjs';
import {clinicalSummary} from './clinical-summary.mjs';
import {networkSign,networkVerify,networkRequestText,networkResponseText} from './network-signatures.mjs';

export const REFERRAL_MAX_BYTES=8*1024*1024;
export function referralVisits(ctx,row){
 const rows=row.health_id?.trim()?ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM patient_records WHERE lower(trim(health_id))=lower(trim(?)) ORDER BY entry_date DESC,created_at DESC').all(row.health_id):[row];
 // Match the source patient-history policy: authorize the referred encounter,
 // then share that identity's visits, never other identities or writable access.
 return rows.map(visit=>{
  const patient=ctx.patientRecordResponse(visit,true),data={};
  for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(patient.data||{}))if((/^(?:scope:(?:registration|triage|medical|nursing|common):|id:|auto:)/.test(key)&&!/(?:photo|password|csrf)/i.test(key))||/^system:(?:nurs|midwife|initialIpdMedications|opdMedications|dailyMonitoringRecords|doctorDailyMedicineOrders|signature:|discharge|medicalRecordCompleted)/.test(key))data[key]=value;
  patient.data=data;delete patient.photo;
  const {problems,allergies,resuscitation}=clinicalSummary(ctx.database,visit);
  return {patient,clinical:{problems,allergies,resuscitation},form:latestReferral(ctx.database,visit.patient_key)};
 });
}

export const linkFile=path.join(os.homedir(),'Borkeo_EMR_Referral_Link','link.json');
const fail=(status,message)=>{throw Object.assign(new Error(message),{status})};
export function linkSettings(config){
  if(config.referralNetworkManaged?.())return null;
  const link=config.referralLink ?? (!config.demoMode && fs.existsSync(linkFile)?JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(linkFile,'utf8')).instances?.[String(config.port)]:null);
  if(!link)return null;
  if(!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(link.secret||'')||!['sender','receiver'].includes(link.role))fail(503,'ការតភ្ជាប់មិនទាន់រួចរាល់');
  const url=new URL(link.peer);
  if(url.protocol!=='http:'||!['127.0.0.1','localhost'].includes(url.hostname)||url.username||url.password||url.pathname!=='/'||url.search||url.hash)fail(503,'ការតភ្ជាប់មិនទាន់រួចរាល់');
  return {...link,peer:url.origin};
}
export function ensureReferralLinkSchema(db){db.sqlite.exec(`
 CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS referral_outbox(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,patient_key TEXT NOT NULL,revision INTEGER NOT NULL,payload TEXT NOT NULL,status TEXT NOT NULL,accepted_at INTEGER,created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,UNIQUE(patient_key,revision)) STRICT;
 CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS referral_inbox(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,payload TEXT NOT NULL,digest TEXT NOT NULL,status TEXT NOT NULL,received_at INTEGER NOT NULL,accepted_at INTEGER,accepted_by TEXT) STRICT;
 CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS referral_link_nonces(nonce TEXT PRIMARY KEY,created_at INTEGER NOT NULL) STRICT;
`);
 for(const table of ['referral_inbox','referral_outbox']){
  const columns=new Set(db.sqlite.prepare('PRAGMA table_info('+table+')').all().map(c=>c.name));
  for(const [name,type] of [['rejection_reason',"TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''"],['rejected_at','INTEGER'],...(table==='referral_inbox'?[['rejected_by','TEXT']]:[])])if(!columns.has(name))db.sqlite.exec('ALTER TABLE '+table+' ADD COLUMN '+name+' '+type);
 }
}
export const digest=value=>crypto.createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(value)).digest('hex');
export const mac=(secret,value)=>crypto.createHmac('sha256',secret).update(value).digest('hex');
const equal=(a,b)=>typeof a==='string'&&typeof b==='string'&&a.length===b.length&&crypto.timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(a),Buffer.from(b));
export function checkEnvelope(db,link,envelope){
 if(!link||!['sender','receiver'].includes(link.role))fail(503,'ការតភ្ជាប់មិនទាន់រួចរាល់');
 const {timestamp,nonce,body,signature}=envelope||{};
 if(!Number.isSafeInteger(timestamp)||Math.abs(Date.now()-timestamp)>60000||!/^[-a-f0-9]{36}$/.test(nonce||'')||!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(signature||'')||!equal(signature,mac(link.secret,JSON.stringify({timestamp,nonce,body}))))fail(403,'មិនមានសិទ្ធិ');
 db.sqlite.prepare('DELETE FROM referral_link_nonces WHERE created_at<?').run(Date.now()-120000);
 if(db.sqlite.prepare('SELECT 1 FROM referral_link_nonces WHERE nonce=?').get(nonce))fail(409,'សំណើដដែល');
 db.sqlite.prepare('INSERT INTO referral_link_nonces VALUES(?,?)').run(nonce,Date.now());
 return body;
}
export async function peerCall(link,body){
 if(typeof link?.invoke==='function')return link.invoke(body);
 if(!link||(link.role!=='sender'&&!(link.role==='receiver'&&body.operation==='history')))fail(503,'ការតភ្ជាប់មិនទាន់រួចរាល់');
 const request={timestamp:Date.now(),nonce:crypto.randomUUID(),body};
 request.signature=mac(link.secret,JSON.stringify(request));
 if(link.networkFrom){request.from=link.networkFrom;request.to=link.networkTo;request.auth=networkSign(link.signingSeed,networkRequestText(request))}
 try{
  const response=await fetch(link.peer+(link.networkFrom?'/api/emr-network/rpc':'/api/referral-link'),{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(request),redirect:'error',signal:AbortSignal.timeout(8000)});
  if(!response.ok)throw Error('peer');
  const maximum=['history','records'].includes(body.operation)?REFERRAL_MAX_BYTES+200000:['search','doctors'].includes(body.operation)?200000:20000;
  const chunks=[];let size=0;for await(const chunk of response.body){size+=chunk.byteLength;if(size>maximum)throw Error('peer');chunks.push(Buffer.from(chunk))}const raw=Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8');
  const result=JSON.parse(raw);
  if(link.networkFrom&&!networkVerify(link.peerVerifyKey,networkResponseText(request.nonce,result.body),result.auth))throw Error('signature');
  if(!equal(result.signature,mac(link.secret,request.nonce+JSON.stringify(result.body))))throw Error('signature');
  return result.body;
 }catch{fail(503,'មិនអាចបញ្ជូនបាន សូមព្យាយាមម្ដងទៀត')}
}
export function receiveReferral(db,body){
 const id=body?.id;
 if(!/^[-a-f0-9]{36}$/.test(id||''))fail(400,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');
 if(body.operation==='deliver'){
  const p=body.payload;
  if(!p||p.id!==id||typeof p.patient?.fullName!=='string'||!p.form?.signature||!Number.isSafeInteger(p.form.revision)||Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(p))>REFERRAL_MAX_BYTES)fail(400,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');
  const prior=db.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM referral_inbox WHERE id=?').get(id);
  if(prior&&prior.digest!==digest(p))fail(409,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');
  if(!prior)db.sqlite.prepare("INSERT INTO referral_inbox(id,payload,digest,status,received_at) VALUES(?,?,?,'PENDING',?)").run(id,JSON.stringify(p),digest(p),Date.now());
 }else if(body.operation!=='status')fail(400,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');
 const row=db.sqlite.prepare('SELECT id,digest,status,accepted_at,rejected_at,rejection_reason FROM referral_inbox WHERE id=?').get(id);
 if(!row)fail(404,'រកមិនឃើញ');
 return {id:row.id,digest:row.digest,status:row.status,acceptedAt:row.accepted_at,rejectedAt:row.rejected_at,rejectionReason:row.rejection_reason};
}

===== END FILE: server/referral-link.mjs =====

import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';

const fail=(status,message)=>{throw Object.assign(new Error(message),{status})};
const uuid=value=>/^[a-f0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{12}$/.test(String(value||''));
const text=(v,max)=>{if(typeof v!=='string'||!v.trim()||v.length>max||/[\u0000-\u0008\u000b\u000c\u000e-\u001f\u007f]/.test(v))fail(400,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');return v.trim()};

const doctors=db=>db.sqlite.prepare("SELECT id,display_name FROM users u WHERE is_active=1 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM user_roles r WHERE r.user_id=u.id AND r.role IN ('DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD')) ORDER BY display_name LIMIT 200").all();
export function mountEmrNetworkRoutes(router){
 const send=(ctx,body)=>ctx.sendJson(ctx.response,200,body,{secure:ctx.config.cookieSecure});
 const audit=(ctx,action,details={})=>ctx.audit(ctx.database,ctx.request,action,{userId:ctx.session?.user_id,details});
 const opts={requireSession:true};
 for(const [url,file,roles] of [['/admin/emr-connections','emr-connections.html',['ADMIN']],['/connected-emrs','connected-emrs.html',REVIEW_ROLES]])router.get(url,ctx=>{role(ctx,roles);return ctx.sendFile(ctx.response,path.join(ctx.config.protectedDirectory,file),'text/html; charset=utf-8',{secure:ctx.config.cookieSecure,request:ctx.request,requestPath:url,review:url==='/connected-emrs'})},opts);
 router.get('/api/emr-network/settings',ctx=>{role(ctx,['ADMIN']);const local=networkLocal(ctx.database);return send(ctx,{local:{id:local.id,name:local.name,enabled:!!local.enabled,managed:!!local.managed,version:local.version,keyHint:'••••'+networkPublicKey(local.signing_seed).slice(-4)},peers:networkPeers(ctx.database).map(publicPeer)})},opts);

  role(ctx,['ADMIN']);const body=await ctx.readJson(ctx.request,3000),local=networkLocal(ctx.database),name=text(body.name,160);

 },opts);

AND version=?').run(crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex'),local.id,local.version);audit(ctx,'EMR_NETWORK_KEY_ROTATED')}
  else audit(ctx,'EMR_NETWORK_KEY_COPIED');

OR (direction=\'OUT\' AND sender_id=?) ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 100').all(ctx.session.user_id,ctx.session.user_id)})},opts);
 router.post('/api/emr-network/messages',async ctx=>{
  role(ctx,DOCTORS);const body=await ctx.readJson(ctx.request,20000),peerId=text(body.peerId,36),id=String(body.id||'');if(!uuid(id)||!uuid(body.recipientId))fail(400,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');

  if(row?.state==='DELIVERED')return send(ctx,{ok:true});

  if(!row)ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('INSERT INTO emr_network_messages VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)').run(id,peerId,'OUT',sender.id,sender.display_name,recipient.id,recipient.display_name,subject,message,'PENDING',Date.now());
  try{const result=await networkCall(ctx,peerId,{operation:'message',id,sender:{id:sender.id,name:sender.display_name},recipientId:recipient.id,subject,body:message});if(result.id!==id)fail(503,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');ctx.database.sqlite.prepare("UPDATE emr_network_messages SET state='DELIVERED' WHERE id=?").run(id)}catch(error){ctx.database.sqlite.prepare("UPDATE emr_network_messages SET state='RETRY' WHERE id=?").run(id);throw error}
  audit(ctx,'EMR_NETWORK_MESSAGE_SENT',{peerId,messageId:id});return send(ctx,{ok:true});
 },opts);
 const rate=new Map();
 router.post('/api/emr-network/rpc',async ctx=>{
  const envelope=await ctx.readJson(ctx.request,REFERRAL_MAX_BYTES+200000),local=networkLocal(ctx.database);
  if(!local.managed||!local.enabled||envelope?.to!==local.id)fail(403,'មិនមានសិទ្ធិ');
  const peer=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM emr_network_peers WHERE instance_id=?').get(String(envelope.from||''));if(!peer?.enabled)fail(403,'មិនមានសិទ្ធិ');
  if(!networkVerify(peer.secret,networkRequestText(envelope),envelope.auth))fail(403,'មិនមានសិទ្ធិ');
  const key=incomingPairKey(local,peer),body=checkEnvelope(ctx.database,{role:'receiver',secret:key},envelope);
  const limit=rate.get(peer.id);if(!limit||Date.now()-limit.start>60000)rate.set(peer.id,{start:Date.now(),count:1});else if(++limit.count>600)fail(429,'មិនអាចផ្ទុកទិន្នន័យបាន');
  let result;
  if(body?.operation==='ping')result={protocol:1,instanceId:local.id,name:local.name,incoming:!!peer.incoming,search:!!peer.search,messages:!!peer.messages};
  else{
   if(!peer.incoming)fail(403,'មិនមានសិទ្ធិ');
   if(['search','records'].includes(body?.operation)&&!peer.search)fail(403,'មិនមានសិទ្ធិ');
   if(['doctors','message'].includes(body?.operation)&&!peer.messages)fail(403,'មិនមានសិទ្ធិ');
   if(['deliver','status'].includes(body?.operation)){
    const prior=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT peer_id FROM referral_inbox WHERE id=?').get(String(body.id||''));if(prior&&prior.peer_id!==peer.id)fail(404,'រកមិនឃើញ');
    ctx.database.sqlite.exec('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');try{result=receiveReferral(ctx.database,body);if(!prior)ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE referral_inbox SET peer_id=? WHERE id=?').run(peer.id,body.id);ctx.database.sqlite.exec('COMMIT')}catch(error){ctx.database.sqlite.exec('ROLLBACK');throw error}
    if(body.operation==='deliver')audit(ctx,'EMR_NETWORK_REFERRAL_RECEIVED',{peerId:peer.id,transferId:body.id});
   }else if(body?.operation==='history'){
    const sent=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM referral_outbox WHERE id=? AND peer_id=?').get(String(body.id||''),peer.id);if(!sent)fail(404,'រកមិនឃើញ');const patient=ctx.database.findPatient(sent.patient_key);if(!patient)fail(404,'រកមិនឃើញ');result={id:sent.id,patientKey:sent.patient_key,visits:referralVisits(ctx,patient)};
   }else if(body?.operation==='search'){
    const q=text(body.q,120),offset=body.offset??0;if(q.length<3||!Number.isSafeInteger(offset)||offset<0||offset>1000000)fail(400,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');const needle='%'+q.replace(/[\\%_]/g,'\\$&')+'%';
    const rows=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare("SELECT * FROM patient_records WHERE full_name LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\' OR health_id LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\' OR phone LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\' ORDER BY entry_date DESC,created_at DESC LIMIT 25 OFFSET ?").all(needle,needle,needle,offset);
    result={patients:rows.map(r=>summary(ctx.patientRecordResponse(r,false))),nextOffset:rows.length===25?offset+25:null};audit(ctx,'EMR_NETWORK_SEARCH_RECEIVED',{peerId:peer.id});
   }else if(body?.operation==='records'){
    const row=ctx.database.findPatient(text(body.patientKey,180));if(!row)fail(404,'រកមិនឃើញ');result={visits:referralVisits(ctx,row),patientKey:row.patient_key,readOnly:true};audit(ctx,'EMR_NETWORK_RECORD_SHARED',{peerId:peer.id,patientKey:row.patient_key});
   }else if(body?.operation==='doctors')result={doctors:doctors(ctx.database)};
   else if(body?.operation==='message'){
    if(!uuid(body.id)||!uuid(body.sender?.id)||!uuid(body.recipientId))fail(400,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');const name=text(body.sender.name,160),subject=text(body.subject,200),message=text(body.body,6000),recipient=doctors(ctx.database).find(d=>d.id===body.recipientId);if(!recipient)fail(404,'រកមិនឃើញ');
    const prior=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM emr_network_messages WHERE id=?').get(body.id);
    if(prior&&(prior.peer_id!==peer.id||prior.direction!=='IN'||prior.sender_id!==body.sender.id||prior.recipient_id!==recipient.id||prior.subject!==subject||prior.body!==message))fail(409,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');
    if(!prior){ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('INSERT INTO emr_network_messages VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)').run(body.id,peer.id,'IN',body.sender.id,name,recipient.id,recipient.display_name,subject,message,'DELIVERED',Date.now());audit(ctx,'EMR_NETWORK_MESSAGE_RECEIVED',{peerId:peer.id,messageId:body.id})}result={id:body.id};
   }else fail(400,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');
  }
  if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(result))>REFERRAL_MAX_BYTES)fail(413,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');
  return send(ctx,{body:result,signature:mac(key,envelope.nonce+JSON.stringify(result)),auth:networkSign(local.signing_seed,networkResponseText(envelope.nonce,result))});
 },{skipCsrf:true});
}

import {networkLocal} from '../emr-network.mjs';
import {referralAccess,latestReferral,referralDefaults,saveReferral} from '../referral-forms.mjs';
import {getESignature} from '../esign.mjs';
export function mountReferralFormRoutes(router){
 router.get('/api/referral-form',async ctx=>{
  const key=String(ctx.url.searchParams.get('patientKey')||''),{row,canEdit}=referralAccess(ctx.database,ctx.session,key,ctx.opdConsultationPaymentConfirmed);
  const defaults=referralDefaults(row);if(networkLocal(ctx.database).managed)defaults.facility=networkLocal(ctx.database).name;
  if(row.department_code==='anc'){
   const visit=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare("SELECT * FROM anc_visits WHERE patient_key=? AND json_extract(referral_json,'$.signed.userId') IS NOT NULL ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1").get(key);
   if(visit){const referral=JSON.parse(visit.referral_json),data=JSON.parse(visit.data_json);Object.assign(defaults,{reason:(referral.reasons||[]).join(' · '),treatment:referral.treatment||'',gestation:String(visit.ga_weeks||''),bp:data.bp||defaults.bp,pulse:data.pulse||defaults.pulse,temperature:data.temperature||defaults.temperature,spo2:data.spo2||defaults.spo2});}
  }
  return ctx.sendJson(ctx.response,200,{form:latestReferral(ctx.database,key),defaults,canEdit,savedSignature:canEdit?getESignature(ctx.database,ctx.session.user_id):null},{secure:ctx.config.cookieSecure});
 });
 router.post('/api/referral-form',async ctx=>{
  const body=await ctx.readJson(ctx.request,220000),key=String(body.patientKey||'');
  const form=saveReferral(ctx.database,ctx.session,key,body,ctx.opdConsultationPaymentConfirmed);
  ctx.audit(ctx.database,ctx.request,'REFERRAL_FORM_SAVED',{userId:ctx.session.user_id,details:{patientKey:key,revision:form.revision,signed:Boolean(form.signature)}});
  return ctx.sendJson(ctx.response,200,{form},{secure:ctx.config.cookieSecure});
 });
 router.post('/api/referral-form/print',async ctx=>{
  const body=await ctx.readJson(ctx.request,2000),key=String(body.patientKey||'');referralAccess(ctx.database,ctx.session,key,ctx.opdConsultationPaymentConfirmed);
  const form=latestReferral(ctx.database,key);if(!form?.signature||form.revision!==body.revision)return ctx.sendJson(ctx.response,409,{error:'សូមរក្សាទុក និងចុះហត្ថលេខាមុនបោះពុម្ព'},{secure:ctx.config.cookieSecure});
  ctx.audit(ctx.database,ctx.request,'REFERRAL_FORM_PRINTED',{userId:ctx.session.user_id,details:{patientKey:key,revision:form.revision}});
  return ctx.sendJson(ctx.response,200,{form},{secure:ctx.config.cookieSecure});
 });
}

import {networkLocal,networkCapabilities,referralNetworkLink} from '../emr-network.mjs';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import {referralAccess,latestReferral} from '../referral-forms.mjs';
import {linkSettings,checkEnvelope,mac,peerCall,receiveReferral,digest,referralVisits,REFERRAL_MAX_BYTES} from '../referral-link.mjs';

const fail=(status,message)=>{throw Object.assign(new Error(message),{status})};
const statusOf=row=>row?{id:row.id,revision:row.revision,status:row.status,acceptedAt:row.accepted_at,peerId:row.peer_id,rejectedAt:row.rejected_at,rejectionReason:row.rejection_reason}:null;
const canReject=ctx=>['DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD','ADMIN','DIRECTOR','VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(ctx.sessionRole(ctx.session));
export function mountReferralLinkRoutes(router){
 const send=(ctx,body)=>ctx.sendJson(ctx.response,200,body,{secure:ctx.config.cookieSecure});
 const receiver=ctx=>{if(networkLocal(ctx.database).managed)return {role:'receiver',network:true};const link=linkSettings(ctx.config);if(link?.role!=='receiver')fail(403,'មិនមានសិទ្ធិ');return link};
 const session=ctx=>{if(!ctx.session||!ctx.sessionRole(ctx.session))fail(401,'សូមចូលប្រើប្រព័ន្ធជាមុន')};
 router.get('/api/referral-capabilities',ctx=>{session(ctx);return send(ctx,networkLocal(ctx.database).managed?networkCapabilities(ctx):{receiver:linkSettings(ctx.config)?.role==='receiver',sender:linkSettings(ctx.config)?.role==='sender'})},{requireSession:true});
 router.post('/api/referral-link',async ctx=>{
  const envelope=await ctx.readJson(ctx.request,REFERRAL_MAX_BYTES+200000),link=linkSettings(ctx.config);
  const body=checkEnvelope(ctx.database,link,envelope);let result;
  if(link.role==='sender'){
   if(body?.operation!=='history')fail(403,'មិនមានសិទ្ធិ');
   const sent=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM referral_outbox WHERE id=?').get(String(body.id||''));if(!sent)fail(404,'រកមិនឃើញ');
   const row=ctx.database.findPatient(sent.patient_key);if(!row)fail(404,'រកមិនឃើញ');
   result={id:sent.id,patientKey:sent.patient_key,visits:referralVisits(ctx,row),reviewedAt:Date.now()};
   if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(result))>REFERRAL_MAX_BYTES)fail(413,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');
   ctx.audit(ctx.database,ctx.request,'REFERRAL_HISTORY_SHARED',{details:{transferId:sent.id,patientKey:sent.patient_key}});
  }else result=receiveReferral(ctx.database,body);
  if(body.operation==='deliver')ctx.audit(ctx.database,ctx.request,'REFERRAL_RECEIVED',{details:{transferId:body.id}});
  return send(ctx,{body:result,signature:mac(link.secret,envelope.nonce+JSON.stringify(result))});
 },{skipCsrf:true}); // Authenticated by timestamped HMAC; browser cookies are never used.
 router.post('/api/referral-send',async ctx=>{
  const body=await ctx.readJson(ctx.request,2000),key=String(body.patientKey||'');
  const {row,canEdit}=referralAccess(ctx.database,ctx.session,key,ctx.opdConsultationPaymentConfirmed);
  if(!canEdit)fail(403,'មិនមានសិទ្ធិ');
  const link=referralNetworkLink(ctx,body.peerId),form=latestReferral(ctx.database,key);
  if(!form?.signature||form.revision!==body.revision)fail(409,'សូមរក្សាទុក និងចុះហត្ថលេខាជាមុន');
  if(link?.role!=='sender')fail(503,'ការតភ្ជាប់មិនទាន់រួចរាល់');
  let transfer=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM referral_outbox WHERE patient_key=? AND revision=?').get(key,form.revision);
  if(transfer&&(transfer.peer_id||'')!==(link.peerId||''))fail(409,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');
  if(!transfer){
   const previous=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM referral_outbox WHERE patient_key=? LIMIT 1').get(key);
   if(previous)fail(409,'បានបញ្ជូនរួចហើយ');
   const id=crypto.randomUUID(),patient={};
   const summary=ctx.patientRecordResponse(row,false);
   for(const key of ['patientKey','fullName','healthId','sex','dateOfBirth','age','phone','equityCard','paymentType','departmentCode','departmentNameKh','serviceMode','entryDate','dischargeDate'])patient[key]=summary[key]||'';
   const payload={id,sourceInstanceId:networkLocal(ctx.database).managed?networkLocal(ctx.database).id:undefined,source:'បរកែវ',patient,form,visits:referralVisits(ctx,row),sentAt:Date.now()};
   if(link.peerId)payload.source=networkLocal(ctx.database).name;
   if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(payload))>REFERRAL_MAX_BYTES)fail(413,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');
   ctx.database.sqlite.prepare("INSERT INTO referral_outbox(id,patient_key,revision,payload,status,created_at,peer_id) VALUES(?,?,?,?,'SENDING',?,?)").run(id,key,form.revision,JSON.stringify(payload),Date.now(),link.peerId||'');
   transfer=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM referral_outbox WHERE id=?').get(id);
  }
  const payload=JSON.parse(transfer.payload);
  try{
   const result=await peerCall(link,{operation:'deliver',id:transfer.id,payload});
   if(result.id!==transfer.id||result.digest!==digest(payload)||!['PENDING','ACCEPTED','REJECTED'].includes(result.status))fail(503,'ទិន្នន័យមិនត្រឹមត្រូវ');
   ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE referral_outbox SET status=?,accepted_at=?,rejected_at=?,rejection_reason=? WHERE id=?').run(result.status,result.acceptedAt||null,result.rejectedAt||null,result.rejectionReason||'',transfer.id);
  }catch(error){ctx.database.sqlite.prepare("UPDATE referral_outbox SET status='RETRY' WHERE id=? AND status NOT IN ('ACCEPTED','REJECTED')").run(transfer.id);throw error}
  ctx.audit(ctx.database,ctx.request,'REFERRAL_SENT',{userId:ctx.session.user_id,details:{patientKey:key,transferId:transfer.id,revision:form.revision}});
  return send(ctx,{transfer:statusOf(ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM referral_outbox WHERE id=?').get(transfer.id))});
 },{requireSession:true});
 router.get('/api/referral-status',async ctx=>{
  const key=String(ctx.url.searchParams.get('patientKey')||'');referralAccess(ctx.database,ctx.session,key,ctx.opdConsultationPaymentConfirmed);
  let row=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM referral_outbox WHERE patient_key=? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1').get(key);
  let unavailable=false;
  if(row&&!['ACCEPTED','REJECTED'].includes(row.status))try{
   if(networkLocal(ctx.database).managed&&!row.peer_id)throw Error('unbound legacy transfer');
   const result=await peerCall(referralNetworkLink(ctx,row.peer_id),{operation:'status',id:row.id});
   if(result.id!==row.id||result.digest!==digest(JSON.parse(row.payload))||!['PENDING','ACCEPTED','REJECTED'].includes(result.status))throw Error('invalid');
   ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('UPDATE referral_outbox SET status=?,accepted_at=?,rejected_at=?,rejection_reason=? WHERE id=?').run(result.status,result.acceptedAt||null,result.rejectedAt||null,result.rejectionReason||'',row.id);
   row=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM referral_outbox WHERE id=?').get(row.id);
  }catch{unavailable=true}
  return send(ctx,{transfer:statusOf(row),unavailable});
 },{requireSession:true});
 router.get('/referrals',ctx=>{session(ctx);receiver(ctx);return ctx.sendFile(ctx.response,path.join(ctx.config.protectedDirectory,'referrals.html'),'text/html; charset=utf-8',{secure:ctx.config.cookieSecure,request:ctx.request,requestPath:'/referrals',review:true})},{requireSession:true});
 router.get('/api/referral-inbox',ctx=>{session(ctx);receiver(ctx);const rows=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM referral_inbox ORDER BY received_at DESC').all();return send(ctx,{canReject:canReject(ctx),referrals:rows.map(row=>{const payload=JSON.parse(row.payload);delete payload.visits;return {id:row.id,status:row.status,receivedAt:row.received_at,acceptedAt:row.accepted_at,rejectionReason:row.rejection_reason,rejectedAt:row.rejected_at,payload}})})},{requireSession:true});
 router.get('/api/referral-history',async ctx=>{
  session(ctx);const link=receiver(ctx),id=String(ctx.url.searchParams.get('id')||'');
  const row=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM referral_inbox WHERE id=?').get(id);if(!row)fail(404,'រកមិនឃើញ');
  const payload=JSON.parse(row.payload);let visits=payload.visits||[],unavailable=false,patientKey=payload.patient.patientKey;
  try{if(link.network&&!row.peer_id)throw Error('unbound legacy transfer');const current=await peerCall(link.network?referralNetworkLink(ctx,row.peer_id):link,{operation:'history',id});if(current.id!==id||!Array.isArray(current.visits))throw Error('invalid');visits=current.visits;patientKey=current.patientKey}catch{unavailable=true}
  // The delivered referral remains immutable even when newer source visits exist.
  for(const visit of visits)if(visit.patient.patientKey===patientKey||visit.form?.id===payload.form.id)visit.form=payload.form;
  if(!visits.length)visits=[{patient:payload.patient,form:payload.form,unavailable:true}];
  ctx.audit(ctx.database,ctx.request,'REFERRAL_HISTORY_VIEWED',{userId:ctx.session.user_id,details:{transferId:id}});
  return send(ctx,{visits,unavailable});
 },{requireSession:true});
 router.get('/api/referral-record-template',ctx=>{session(ctx);receiver(ctx);return send(ctx,{html:fs.readFileSync(path.join(ctx.config.protectedDirectory,'EMR.html'),'utf8')})},{requireSession:true});
 router.post('/api/referral-accept',async ctx=>{
  session(ctx);receiver(ctx);const body=await ctx.readJson(ctx.request,2000);
  const row=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM referral_inbox WHERE id=?').get(String(body.id||''));if(!row)fail(404,'រកមិនឃើញ');
  if(row.status==='REJECTED')fail(409,'លិខិតបញ្ជូននេះបានបដិសេធរួចហើយ');
  const changed=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare("UPDATE referral_inbox SET status='ACCEPTED',accepted_at=?,accepted_by=? WHERE id=? AND status='PENDING'").run(Date.now(),ctx.session.user_id,row.id);
  if(changed.changes)ctx.audit(ctx.database,ctx.request,'REFERRAL_ACCEPTED',{userId:ctx.session.user_id,details:{transferId:row.id}});
  return send(ctx,{ok:true});
 },{requireSession:true});
 router.post('/api/referral-reject',async ctx=>{
  session(ctx);receiver(ctx);if(!canReject(ctx))fail(403,'មិនមានសិទ្ធិ');
  const body=await ctx.readJson(ctx.request,6000),reason=typeof body.reason==='string'?body.reason.trim():'';
  if(!reason||reason.length>1000||/[\u0000-\u0008\u000b\u000c\u000e-\u001f\u007f]/.test(reason))fail(400,'សូមបញ្ចូលមូលហេតុបដិសេធ');
  const row=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare('SELECT * FROM referral_inbox WHERE id=?').get(String(body.id||''));if(!row)fail(404,'រកមិនឃើញ');
  if(row.status==='ACCEPTED')fail(409,'លិខិតបញ្ជូននេះបានទទួលរួចហើយ');
  const changed=ctx.database.sqlite.prepare("UPDATE referral_inbox SET status='REJECTED',rejection_reason=?,rejected_at=?,rejected_by=? WHERE id=? AND status='PENDING'").run(reason,Date.now(),ctx.session.user_id,row.id);
  if(changed.changes)ctx.audit(ctx.database,ctx.request,'REFERRAL_REJECTED',{userId:ctx.session.user_id,details:{transferId:row.id}});
  return send(ctx,{ok:true});
 },{requireSession:true});
}

const clinicalFormTypes = new Set(CLINICAL_FORM_TYPES);
const clinicalWriteRoles = new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR', 'DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD', 'NURSE', 'MIDWIFE']);
const clinicalFormReadRoles = new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR', 'DIRECTOR', 'REGISTRATION', 'TRIAGE', 'DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD', 'NURSE', 'MIDWIFE']);
const clinicalFormWriteRoles = new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR', 'DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD']);

function landingPathForRole(role, wardCode = '') {
  role = String(role || '').toUpperCase();
  if (['CLERK','ACCOUNTANT','VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(role)) return '/billing';
  if (role === 'LAB') return '/lab';
  if (role === 'IMAGING') return '/imaging';
  if (role === 'TRIAGE') return '/triage';
  if (role === 'REGISTRATION') return '/emr?view=register';
  if (role === 'NURSE' && String(wardCode || '').toLowerCase() === 'opd') return '/opd-paraclinic-nurse';
  if (role === 'WARD_HEAD' || role === 'CHIEF_WARD') return '/ward-medicines';
  if (role === 'NURSE' || role === 'MIDWIFE') return '/departments';
  return '/home';
}

function messagingVisibility(session) {
  return { userId: session.user_id, role: sessionRole(session), wardCode: sessionWard(session), username: session.username };
}

function messagingRoleAllowed(role) {
  return new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR','REGISTRATION','TRIAGE','DOCTOR','NURSE','MIDWIFE','CHIEF_WARD','WARD_HEAD','CLERK','ACCOUNTANT','LAB','IMAGING','PHARMACY']).has(String(role || '').toUpperCase());
}

function destinationExists(type, code='') {
  type = String(type || '').toUpperCase();
  if (['LAB','IMAGING','PHARMACY','ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR','TRIAGE','REGISTRATION','CLERK','ACCOUNTANT'].includes(type)) return true;
  if (type !== 'WARD') return false;
  return WARDS.some(ward => ward.code === String(code || '').toLowerCase());
}

function automaticWardMessage(database, actor, patient, subject, body, options = {}) {
  const wardCode = String(options.wardCode || patientWorkWard(patient) || '').toLowerCase();
  if (!wardCode) return null;
  return createSystemMessage(database, {
    senderUserId: actor.userId,
    senderRole: actor.role,
    senderWardCode: actor.wardCode || '',
    destinationType: 'WARD',
    destinationCode: wardCode,
    destinationRole: options.destinationRole || '',
    subject,
    body,
    patientKey: patient.patient_key,
  });
}

function notifyOpdDoctorsPaymentCompleted(database, session, patient, bill, payments = []) {
  if (String(patient?.service_mode || '').toLowerCase() !== 'opd') return null;
  const money = (amount, currency) => currency === 'USD'
    ? `$${Number(amount || 0).toFixed(2)}`
    : `${Math.round(Number(amount || 0)).toLocaleString('en-US')} ៛`;
  const paid = ['KHR','USD'].map(currency => {
    const amount = Number(bill?.summary?.[currency]?.paid || 0);
    return amount > 0 ? money(amount, currency) : '';
  }).filter(Boolean).join(' + ') || payments.map(item => money(item.amount, item.currency)).join(' + ');
  const waived = ['KHR','USD'].map(currency => {
    const amount = Number(bill?.summary?.[currency]?.waived || 0);
    return amount > 0 ? money(amount, currency) : '';
  }).filter(Boolean).join(' + ');
  const settlement = paid || (waived ? `Fully waived: ${waived}` : 'Invoice settled');
  const name = patient.full_name || patient.case_number || patient.patient_key;
  const invoice = bill?.invoice?.invoiceNo || bill?.invoice?.id || '—';
  return automaticWardMessage(database,
    { userId:session.user_id, role:sessionRole(session), wardCode:sessionWard(session) },
    patient,
    'Cashier payment completed',
    `${name} · invoice ${invoice} · ${settlement}. The OPD invoice is settled; the patient may continue with the OPD doctor.`,
    { wardCode:'opd', destinationRole:'DOCTOR' });
}

function admissionWardCode(update) {
  const room = String(update?.roomCode || '').toLowerCase();
  const department = String(update?.departmentCode || '').toLowerCase();
  if (room === 'medical') return 'medicine';
  if (room === 'surgical') return 'surgery';
  if (['emergency','pediatric','gynecology','obstetric','tb','infectious','icu','ncu'].includes(room)) return room;
  if (department === 'gyneco-obstetric') return room === 'obstetric' ? 'obstetric' : 'gynecology';
  return department;
}

function notifyTriageReferral(database, session, patient) {
  const department = String(patient.department_code || '').toLowerCase();
  const name = patient.full_name || patient.case_number || patient.patient_key;
  const actor = { userId: session.user_id, role: sessionRole(session), wardCode: sessionWard(session) };
  const getWardKh = (code) => {
    try {
      const w = database.findWard?.(code);
      if (w?.nameKh) return w.nameKh;
    } catch (_) {}
    const map = {
      medicine: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺទូទៅ',
      surgery: 'ផ្នែកវះកាត់',
      pediatric: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺកុមារ',
      pediatrics: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺកុមារ',
      maternity: 'ផ្នែកសម្ភព',
      obstetric: 'ផ្នែកសម្ភព',
      gynecology: 'ផ្នែករោគស្ត្រី',
      emergency: 'ផ្នែកសង្គ្រោះបន្ទាន់',
      tb: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺរបេង',
      infectious: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺឆ្លង',
      dental: 'ផ្នែកធ្មេញ',
      mental: 'ផ្នែកសុខភាពផ្លូវចិត្ត',
      opd: 'ផ្នែកពិគ្រោះក្រៅ'
    };
    return map[code] || (code ? `ផ្នែក ${code}` : 'ផ្នែកសម្រាកពេទ្យ');
  };
  const send = (wardCode, destinationRole) => {
    const targetDept = getWardKh(wardCode);
    const msg = `អ្នកជំងឺឈ្មោះ ${name} ត្រូវបានបញ្ជូនពីផ្នែកទ្រីយ៉ាមកផ្នែក ${targetDept.replace(/^ផ្នែក\s*/, '')} សូមពិនិត្យមើល និងរៀបចំជូនអ្នកជំងឺ`;
    return automaticWardMessage(
      database, actor, patient,
      msg,
      msg,
      { wardCode, destinationRole },
    );
  };
  if (department === 'gyneco-obstetric') {
    send('gynecology', 'NURSE');
    send('obstetric', 'MIDWIFE');
    return;
  }
  const wardCode = patientWorkWard(patient) || department;
  if (!wardCode || wardCode === 'opd') return;
  send(wardCode, wardCode === 'obstetric' ? 'MIDWIFE' : 'NURSE');
}

// v42.4: behind a hosting proxy (TRUST_PROXY=true) the client address is the
// last address in X-Forwarded-For (added by the proxy itself; earlier entries
// can be sent by the client). Login lockout, the audit log and the record view
// log all use this address. Without TRUST_PROXY the header is ignored.
function requestIp(request) {
  if (request.borkeoTrustProxy) {
    const forwarded = String(request.headers['x-forwarded-for'] || '').split(',').map(part => part.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if (forwarded.length) return forwarded[forwarded.length - 1];
  }
  return request.socket.remoteAddress ?? 'unknown';
}

const htmlTemplateCache = new Map();
const staticFileCache = new Map();
const staticCompressedCache = new Map();

function compressIfNeeded(request, response, buffer, contentType) {
  if (!buffer || buffer.length < 512) return buffer;
  const isCompressible = /^(text\/|application\/json|application\/javascript|font\/ttf|image\/svg\+xml)/i.test(contentType);
  if (!isCompressible) return buffer;

  const accept = String(request?.headers?.['accept-encoding'] || '');
  if (/\bgzip\b/i.test(accept)) {
    response.setHeader('Content-Encoding', 'gzip');
    response.setHeader('Vary', 'Accept-Encoding');
    return zlib.gzipSync(buffer, { level: 6 });
  }
  if (/\bdeflate\b/i.test(accept)) {
    response.setHeader('Content-Encoding', 'deflate');
    response.setHeader('Vary', 'Accept-Encoding');
    return zlib.deflateSync(buffer, { level: 6 });
  }
  return buffer;
}

function getProcessedHtml(filePath, contentType, security, APP_VERSION) {
  const stat = fs.statSync(filePath);
  const cacheKey = `${filePath}:${stat.mtimeMs}:${APP_VERSION}:${security?.requestPath || ''}`;
  const cached = htmlTemplateCache.get(filePath);
  if (cached && cached.cacheKey === cacheKey) {
    return cached.bytes;
  }
  let html = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');
  html = html.replace(/(<span\b[^>]*\bdata-app-version\b[^>]*>)[^<]*(<\/span>)/g, (_, open, close) => `${open}v${APP_VERSION}${close}`);
  html = html.replace(/Borkeo EMR v\d+\.\d+/g, `Borkeo EMR v${APP_VERSION}`);
  html = html.replace(/<head\b[^>]*>/i, head => head + '<style>.emr-language-switch, [data-emr-language], #langBtn, .lang-btn, body #emr-site-banner .portal-account-actions .emr-language-switch, #emr-session-control .portal-account-actions .emr-language-switch, .portal-account-actions .emr-language-switch { display: none !important; visibility: hidden !important; opacity: 0 !important; pointer-events: none !important; width: 0 !important; height: 0 !important; max-width: 0 !important; max-height: 0 !important; margin: 0 !important; padding: 0 !important; border: 0 !important; position: absolute !important; left: -9999px !important; clip: rect(0,0,0,0) !important; }</style><script src="/public/emr-language-lock.js?v=3760"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/emr-required-fields.css?v=3770"><script src="/public/emr-required-fields.js?v=3770"></script>');
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/design-system.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/components.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/app-shell.css?v=3980"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/patient-banner.css?v=4100"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/soap-note.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/clinical-data.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/worklists-feedback.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/interface-theme.css?v=1970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/npca.css?v=4280"><script src="/public/npca-widget.js?v=4280" defer></script><script src="/public/npca-forms.js?v=4280" defer></script><script src="/public/components.js?v=3970" defer></script><script src="/public/nav-config.js?v=4970" defer></script><script src="/public/app-shell.js?v=4970" defer></script><script src="/public/patient-banner.js?v=4170" defer></script><script type="module" src="/public/referral-form.mjs?v=4970"></script><script src="/public/soap-note.js?v=3970" defer></script><script src="/public/clinical-data.js?v=3970" defer></script><script src="/public/worklists-feedback.js?v=3970" defer></script><script src="/public/interface-theme.js?v=1970" defer></script></head>');
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/shared-controls.css?v=1970"><script src="/public/request-badges.js?v=1970" defer></script></head>');
  // v42.3: one medication unit table for every page (old Khmer unit labels shown as mg, mL, mcg, g, IU).
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<script src="/public/record-access.js?v=4570" defer></script></head>');
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<script src="/public/medicine-units.js?v=4230"></script></head>');
  // v42.5: one medication route table for every page (new lines: IV, IM, SC and the simple routes).
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<script src="/public/medication-routes.js?v=4250"></script></head>');
  // v42.3: medicine name search in the prescription forms (medical record page only).
  if(path.basename(filePath)==='EMR.html')html=html.replace('</head>','<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/medicine-autocomplete.css?v=4250"><script src="/public/medicine-autocomplete.js?v=4250" defer></script><script src="/public/medicine-dose-check.js?v=4250" defer></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/maternal.css?v=4260"><script src="/public/maternal-emr.js?v=4260" defer></script></head>');
  if(path.basename(filePath)==='EMR.html')html=html.replace('</head>','<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/emr-encounter-editor.css?v=3800"><script src="/public/emr-encounter-editor.js?v=4930" defer></script></head>');
  // v41.0: structured allergy record, problem list and nursing demographics (medical record page only).
  if(path.basename(filePath)==='EMR.html')html=html.replace('</head>','<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/clinical-summary.css?v=4100"><script src="/public/clinical-summary.js?v=4100" defer></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/display-rules.css?v=4110"><script src="/public/display-rules.js?v=4110" defer></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/medication-safety.css?v=4120"><script src="/public/medication-safety.js?v=4120" defer></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/periop-forms.css?v=4160"><script src="/public/periop-forms.js?v=4160" defer></script><script src="/public/fluid-balance.js?v=4160" defer></script><script src="/public/early-warning.js?v=4160" defer></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/patient-documents.css?v=4170"><script src="/public/patient-documents.js?v=4170" defer></script></head>');
  // v43.3: modern clinical consultation workspace and integrated patient record (EMR.html only).
  if(path.basename(filePath)==='EMR.html')html=html.replace('</head>','<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/emr-consultation-workspace.css?v=4330"><script src="/public/emr-consultation-workspace.js?v=4330" defer></script><script src="/public/emr-shared-encounter.js?v=4700" defer></script></head>');
  html = html.replace('</head>', `<meta name="borkeo-version" content="${APP_VERSION}"></head>`);
  // New text without approved Khmer is highlighted unless SHOW_UNTRANSLATED=0 (public/new-text.js).
  const showUntranslated = globalThis.process?.env?.SHOW_UNTRANSLATED === '0' ? '0' : '1';
  html = html.replace('</head>', `<meta name="borkeo-show-untranslated" content="${showUntranslated}"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/new-text.css?v=4060"><script src="/public/new-text-data.js?v=4060"></script><script src="/public/new-text.js?v=4060"></script></head>`);
  if (!html.includes('/public/emr-language.js')) {
    const langVersion = APP_VERSION.split('.').slice(0, 2).join('') + '0';
    const languageAssets = `<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/emr-language.css?v=${langVersion}"><script src="/public/emr-language-data.js?v=1750" defer></script><script src="/public/emr-language.js?v=${langVersion}" defer></script>`;
    html = html.replace('</head>', `${languageAssets}</head>`);
  }
  html = html.replace(/(\/public\/[^"'\s<>]+\?v=[^"'\s<>]+)/g, asset => `${asset}&build=${APP_VERSION}`);
  if (path.basename(filePath) === 'stock.html') {
    const reqPath = String(security?.requestPath || '');
    if (reqPath.startsWith('/lab')) {
      html = html.replace(/<section class="imaging-toolbar[\s\S]*?<\/section>/g, '');
      html = html.replace(/<link[^>]*imaging\.css[^>]*>/g, '');
    } else if (reqPath.startsWith('/imaging')) {
      html = html.replace(/<section class="lab-toolbar[\s\S]*?<\/section>/g, '');
      html = html.replace(/<link[^>]*lab\.css[^>]*>/g, '');
    }
  }
  const bytes = Buffer.from(html, 'utf8');
  htmlTemplateCache.set(filePath, { cacheKey, bytes });
  return bytes;
}

function sendJson(response, status, body, security = {}) {
  applySecurityHeaders(response, security);
  const rawBytes = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(body));
  const req = response.req;
  const compressedBytes = compressIfNeeded(req, response, rawBytes, 'application/json');
  response.writeHead(status, {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8',
    'Content-Length': compressedBytes.length,
    'Cache-Control': 'no-store',
  });
  response.end(compressedBytes);
}

function redirect(response, location, security = {}) {
  applySecurityHeaders(response, security);
  response.writeHead(302, { Location: location, 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' });
  response.end();
}

function sendFile(response, filePath, contentType, security = {}, cache = false) {
  try {
    const req = response.req;
    const stat = fs.statSync(filePath);
    const mtimeMs = stat.mtimeMs;
    const isHtml = path.basename(filePath) !== 'print-preview.html' && contentType.startsWith('text/html;') &&
        (filePath.includes(`${path.sep}protected${path.sep}`) || path.basename(filePath) === 'login.html');

    let bodyBytes = null;
    if (isHtml) {
      bodyBytes = getProcessedHtml(filePath, contentType, security, APP_VERSION);
    }

    const etag = `W/"${stat.size.toString(16)}-${Math.floor(mtimeMs).toString(16)}${isHtml ? `-${APP_VERSION}` : ''}"`;
    const ifNoneMatch = req?.headers?.['if-none-match'];

    if (ifNoneMatch && ifNoneMatch === etag) {
      applySecurityHeaders(response, security);
      response.writeHead(304, {
        'ETag': etag,
        'Cache-Control': cache ? 'public, max-age=86400, stale-while-revalidate=604800' : 'no-cache',
      });
      return response.end();
    }

    if (!bodyBytes) {
      const cached = staticFileCache.get(filePath);
      if (cached && cached.mtimeMs === mtimeMs) {
        bodyBytes = cached.bytes;
      } else if (stat.size <= 2 * 1024 * 1024) {
        bodyBytes = fs.readFileSync(filePath);
        staticFileCache.set(filePath, { mtimeMs, bytes: bodyBytes });
      }
    }

    applySecurityHeaders(response, security);
    response.setHeader('ETag', etag);

    const reqUrl = req?.url || '';
    const hasVersionQuery = reqUrl.includes('?v=') || reqUrl.includes('&build=');
    const isAsset = filePath.includes(`${path.sep}public${path.sep}`) && !isHtml;
    const cacheControlHeader = (cache || (isAsset && hasVersionQuery))
      ? 'public, max-age=86400, stale-while-revalidate=604800'
      : (cache ? 'private, max-age=3600' : 'no-cache');

    response.setHeader('Cache-Control', cacheControlHeader);
    response.setHeader('Content-Type', contentType);

    if (bodyBytes) {
      const accept = String(req?.headers?.['accept-encoding'] || '');
      const enc = /\bgzip\b/i.test(accept) ? 'gzip' : (/\bdeflate\b/i.test(accept) ? 'deflate' : '');
      let finalBytes = bodyBytes;
      if (enc && isAsset && bodyBytes.length >= 512) {
        const cacheKey = `${filePath}:${mtimeMs}:${enc}`;
        let cachedCompressed = staticCompressedCache.get(cacheKey);
        if (!cachedCompressed) {
          cachedCompressed = enc === 'gzip' ? zlib.gzipSync(bodyBytes, { level: 6 }) : zlib.deflateSync(bodyBytes, { level: 6 });
          staticCompressedCache.set(cacheKey, cachedCompressed);
        }
        response.setHeader('Content-Encoding', enc);
        response.setHeader('Vary', 'Accept-Encoding');
        finalBytes = cachedCompressed;
      } else {
        finalBytes = compressIfNeeded(req, response, bodyBytes, contentType);
      }
      response.setHeader('Content-Length', finalBytes.length);
      response.writeHead(200);
      return response.end(finalBytes);
    }

    response.setHeader('Content-Length', stat.size);
    response.writeHead(200);
    fs.createReadStream(filePath).pipe(response);
  } catch {
    sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'Not found.' }, security);
  }
}

function sendBuffer(response, bytes, contentType, security = {}) {
  applySecurityHeaders(response, security);
  const req = response.req;
  const finalBytes = compressIfNeeded(req, response, bytes, contentType);
  response.writeHead(200, {
    'Content-Type': contentType,
    'Content-Length': finalBytes.length,
    'Cache-Control': 'no-store',
    'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff',
  });
  response.end(finalBytes);
}

async function readJson(request, maximumBytes) {
  const contentType = String(request.headers['content-type'] ?? '').split(';')[0].trim();
  if (contentType !== 'application/json') {
    const error = new Error('Content-Type must be application/json.');
    error.status = 415;
    throw error;
  }
  let size = 0;
  const chunks = [];
  for await (const chunk of request) {
    size += chunk.length;
    if (size > maximumBytes) {
      const error = new Error('Request body is too large.');
      error.status = 413;
      throw error;
    }
    chunks.push(chunk);
  }
  try { return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8')); }
  catch {
    const error = new Error('Request body is not valid JSON.');
    error.status = 400;
    throw error;
  }
}

async function readBinary(request, maximumBytes) {
  let size = 0;
  const chunks = [];
  for await (const chunk of request) {
    size += chunk.length;
    if (size > maximumBytes) {
      const error = new Error('Attachment is too large.');
      error.status = 413;
      throw error;
    }
    chunks.push(chunk);
  }
  return Buffer.concat(chunks);
}

function safeAttachmentName(value) {
  const name = String(value || 'attachment').replace(/[\\/\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/g, '_').trim().slice(0, 180);
  return name || 'attachment';
}

function requireContext(session) {
  const role = sessionRole(session);
  if (!role) {
    const error = new Error('Choose a working role and ward first.');
    error.status = 409;
    throw error;
  }
  return role;
}

function orderActorAllowed(role, modality) {
  if (['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(role)) return true;
  if (modality === 'LAB') return role === 'LAB';
  return role === 'IMAGING';
}

function labWardWorkflowRole(role) {
  return ['NURSE','MIDWIFE','DOCTOR'].includes(String(role || '').toUpperCase());
}

function sessionFor(request, database) {
  const token = parseCookies(request.headers.cookie)[SESSION_COOKIE];
  if (!token) return null;
  const hash = tokenHash(token);
  const session = database.findSession(hash);
  const now = Date.now();
  if (!session || session.expires_at <= now || session.is_active !== 1) {
    if (session) database.deleteSession(hash);
    return null;
  }
  if (now - session.last_seen_at > 60_000) database.touchSession(hash, now);
  const context = readSessionContext(database, hash);
  return { ...session, ...context, rawToken: token, tokenHash: hash };
}

function sameOrigin(request, config) {
